   502	
   503	**Repo-side tests.** Raven uses `node --test "test/**/*.test.mjs"` with a `pretest` build; there is no Vitest. Playwright and `pngjs` are optional dependencies, so every new capability degrades gracefully when absent and each has a test asserting the degraded message rather than a throw.
   504	
   505	---
   506	
   507	## 12. MCP tool surface
   508	
   509	Every entry must be added to `TOOL_ACCESS` or `toolAnnotations()` throws `"Missing MCP tool classification"` at registration (`src/index.ts:2151`). **`TOOL_ACCESS` is a static name-keyed map read once at registration — there is no per-call classification, so a tool that can write is classified `destructive` unconditionally.** All seven go in `REMOTE_GATED_TOOLS` to preserve the frozen 45-tool anonymous surface (§6). That keeps the golden hash intact but still moves the local count 105 → 112 and the gated count 60 → 67 across the three phases; each phase updates the three `src/index.ts` count comments and the six tests that assert them (§13, Phase 0).
   510	
   511	| Tool | Input | Output | Access | Phase |
   512	|---|---|---|---|---|
   513	| `compose_build_prompt` | **required** `{intent, project_dir, profile}`; **optional** `{skeleton?, reference_url?, session_id?, ref_ids?, surface?, project?, design_file_path?, inventory_source?: "auto"\|"design-md"\|"registry"\|"scan"\|"none", components?}` — each required argument is required because a named input's real signature demands it (§9 Inputs, right-hand column) | `{prompt, grounding{design_md, design_md_resolved_via: "design_file_path"\|"source-config"\|"default", token_count, inventory_source, component_count, binding_resolved: boolean, decisions_scope, decisions_consulted[], contested_decisions[]}, skeleton, skeleton_required: boolean, bindings[], gaps[], acceptance[]}` | readOnly, **and truthfully so**: it reads the decision store directly via `listActiveDecisions()` / `listDecisions("contested")` rather than through the `decision_list` MCP tool, because that tool is classified `destructive` (`src/index.ts:2080`) and logs a consultation to `consultations.jsonl` on every call with a non-empty result (`src/index.ts:6983`). Returns a string; no `output_path` | 0 |
   514	| `start_critique_session` | `{project, surface, brief, profile?, session_id?}` | `{session_id, panel_url, state}` | destructive | 1 |
   515	| `get_critique_session` | `{session_id, since_seq?, timeout_ms?}` | `{state, references[], annotations[], next_seq}` | readOnly | 1 |
   516	| `close_critique_session` | `{session_id, mode:"extract"\|"discard"}` | `{extraction_prompt}` | destructive | 1 |
   517	| `record_critique_results` | `{session_id, extraction_json, write:("references"\|"taste_decision_drafts"\|"taste_rules")[], confirm?}` | `{written{}, proposed_rules[], rejected[]}` | destructive | 1 |
   518	| `capture_reference` | `{session_id?, url, app, owner:"self"\|"third-party", surface?, profile?, viewport?, theme?, note?, interactions?}` | `{ref_id, image_path, traits, quality{score,flags}, tagging_prompt}` | destructive; `TOOL_OPEN_WORLD`; `REMOTE_URL_GUARDED_TOOLS` | 2 |
   519	| `search_references` | `{query?, surface?, interaction?, screen_type?, owner?, limit?}` | `{results:[{record, score, why}], total, corpus_size}` | readOnly | 2 |
   520	
   521	Bridge routes added in Phase 1, key-gated alongside `/grab`: `GET /critique` (panel HTML), `GET /reference/<ref_id>.png` (media), `POST /critique` (annotation).
   522	
   523	`compose_build_prompt`'s schema is the one above and every example call in this document is valid against it. The three required fields are not preference: `intent` names the build, `project_dir` is the only thing that can resolve a DESIGN.md path for `read_design_md`/`inventory_design_system`/`diff_design_system` (all of which throw without a path — §9), and `profile` is the only thing that can resolve a `TasteProfile` (`getTasteProfile` throws on a missing name, and there is no default profile) and therefore the prohibitions block. Everything else is optional and degrades to a stated gap rather than a guess: no resolvable binding → no `design_notes`/`voice_note`/`build_hints`, `binding_resolved:false`, and a `## Gaps` line. `project_dir` does **not** scope the Decision Graph — that store is a module-global resolved from the environment or `process.cwd()` and cannot be pointed elsewhere per call, so `grounding.decisions_scope` reports where the decisions actually came from and flags a mismatch (§9, with the upgrade path). `skeleton` supplied → Structure/States/Content/Motion are bound and emitted, `skeleton_required: false`; `skeleton` absent → grounding block only, `skeleton_required: true`, and the prompt's first line instructs the agent to derive the tree from its reference and re-submit. `reference_url` is accepted for provenance and for the Phase 0 path where no `ReferenceCapture` is bound yet; it is never fetched by the composer.
   524	
   525	`search_references` returns records and paths, never base64 pixels — matching `audit_url`'s discipline and staying well inside `api/mcp.js`'s `MAX_BODY_BYTES = 400_000`, which one screenshot would bust.
   526	
   527	---
   528	
   529	## 13. Build plan by phase
   530	
   531	### Phase 0 — the loop with zero corpus (~3 days)
   532	
   533	`compose_build_prompt` alone: composes `read_design_md` tokens + `inventory_design_system` + the surface's `design_notes` + every active `TasteRule.negative_prompt` + matching `build_hints` from `TECHNIQUE_RECIPES`, plus the project's decisions read **directly from the store** — `decisionGraphStore.listActiveDecisions()` for active ones and `listDecisions("contested")` for contested ones, the latter routed into `## Gaps / decisions for you`. It does **not** call the `decision_list` MCP tool: that tool is classified `destructive` (`src/index.ts:2080`) and appends to `consultations.jsonl` via `recordConsultation` whenever the result is non-empty (`src/index.ts:6983`, `src/decision-graph.ts:701`), which would make a `readOnly` annotation false; and it cannot return active and contested decisions together — `status` selects exactly one status, and the no-status path returns active decisions plus, when `include_candidates:true`, candidates (`src/index.ts:6972-6977`), so contested decisions never appear on it.
   534	
   535	**Its arguments are derived from its inputs, not chosen.** Required `{intent, project_dir, profile}`; optional `{skeleton, reference_url, session_id, ref_ids, surface, project, design_file_path, inventory_source, components}`. §9's Inputs table states per-input which argument feeds it and why the tool throws without it. The short version of the three rounds of review that produced it: `read_design_md` requires a `path`, `inventory_design_system` throws `"Provide project_dir or design_file_path"`, `getTasteProfile` throws on an unknown or absent name with no default, and `resolveSurfaceBinding` needs a profile name plus a project *name* — so an intent-only call could open no DESIGN.md, load no rules, and resolve no binding, and would have emitted a confidently empty `## Prohibitions` block.
   536	
   537	The reference is named by `reference_url` or by an existing bound `ReferenceCapture`, is used for provenance, and is **never fetched** by the composer. **It is not compared with `referenceDeltas()`** — that function takes the *built* target's `PageTraits` (`src/taste-fidelity.ts:448`) and Phase 0 has no built target at all: no HTML, no URL, no screenshot, no traits. It belongs to §11's post-build verification. Where compose-time intent needs checking against a reference, the function whose actual job that is, is `checkBindingConsistency(design_notes, references)` (`src/taste.ts:1169`) — already what `bind_taste_surface` calls (`src/index.ts:7389`).
   538	
   539	`project_dir` roots the file reads and (via `basename`) the binding lookup; it does not scope the Decision Graph, whose store location is a module global. That named limitation and its upgrade path are in §9. No corpus, no UI, no new dependency.
   540	
   541	**It does not derive the skeleton, and that is the point of the small estimate.** Deriving a Structure/States tree from a reference needs a model looking at the reference; Raven has neither a model nor the reference's pixels — a bound `ReferenceCapture` is a URL, a prose `liked` line, aggregate `PageTraits`, and a timestamp (`src/taste.ts:92`), and `PageTraits` is page-level aggregate metrics (`src/capture.ts:285-303`), not a node tree. The agent calling the tool is already looking at the reference. So: `skeleton` is an optional caller-supplied argument. With one, the composer lints it (colorless/typeless/sizeless), binds archetypes to the project's real components and `emphasis`/`density` to real token ramps, and emits the full template. Without one, it emits the grounding half — Tokens, Prohibitions, Gaps, Acceptance criteria — sets `skeleton_required: true`, and instructs the agent to derive Structure/States from the reference and call again. Anything else would mean adding an LLM dependency to a server that has deliberately never had one (`ingest_transcript` is the standing precedent).
   542	
   543	- **Files, and the list is longer than the code.** New `src/reference-prompt.ts` — the composer **and** the `Skeleton` / `StructureNode` / `StateMachine` / `ContentSlot` / `MotionSpec` / `Provenance` types (§9); no separate `src/pattern-skeleton.ts`. `src/index.ts`: one `server.tool`, one `TOOL_ACCESS` entry (`toolAnnotations()` throws `"Missing MCP tool classification"` without it, §12), one `REMOTE_GATED_TOOLS` entry — **and the three count comments those constants are documented by**, because the tool counts are a frozen contract, not commentary.
   544	- **The tool count is a frozen contract, and one gated tool moves two of the three numbers.** Registering `compose_build_prompt` as remote-gated takes the local surface **105 → 106** and the gated set **60 → 61**. The anonymous remote surface stays at **45** and the `GOLDEN_45_HASH` is untouched — which is the whole reason it is gated (§6). Source constants to update: the gated/remote/local comment block at `src/index.ts:1851-1857` ("60 gated tools", "45 stateless", "from 105 local tools"), the `buildServer` header comment at `:2159-2170` ("all 105 local tools", "serve only the 45 stateless remote-safe tools", "gate off the 60 gated tools", "all 105"), and `main()`'s comment at `:7768-7771` ("serves all 105 tools"). Six existing tests assert these and all six must move in the same change:
   545	  - `test/taste-remote-full.test.mjs:81-93` — test *name* embeds "stdio = 105"; `:93` asserts `105`.
   546	  - `test/grab-bridge.test.mjs:886-891` — `:886` asserts `105`, `:891` asserts `45`.
   547	  - `test/decision-import.test.mjs:477-487` — `:482` asserts `105`, `:484`/`:487` the 45 + golden hash.
   548	  - `test/design-review.test.mjs:861-873` — `:863` asserts `105`, and `:870-873` **regex-match the `src/index.ts` comment strings themselves** (`/FRESH McpServer with all 105 local tools/`, `/gate off the 60 gated tools/`, `/all 105\./`). This is the test that makes the comments a contract rather than prose: changing the code without the comments fails here, and vice versa.
   549	  - `test/audit-dispatch.test.mjs:223-228` — `:226` asserts `105`.
   550	  - `test/redis-taste-store.test.mjs:147-150` — `:150` asserts `105`.
   551	
   552	  This is a **standing tax on every future tool**, and it is deliberate: it is what has kept the remote surface hash-frozen and the documented counts honest across every prior addition. It is roughly a day of the three-day estimate — not because the edits are hard, but because they are mechanical, easy to half-do, and fail loudly rather than silently. Phases 1–2 register six more tools and pay it once each.
   553	- **Test:** `test/reference-prompt.test.mjs` asserts the composed prompt contains every active `negative_prompt`, every `design_notes` key, at least one token `cssVar` from the fixture DESIGN.md, and **zero hex/px literals** in the skeleton section; that a fixture `contested` decision appears under `## Gaps / decisions for you` and an `active` one under `## Prohibitions`; the no-skeleton branch — `skeleton_required: true`, no `## Structure` / `## States` / `## Content` section, and the derive-and-re-submit instruction present; and the four failure paths the schema exists to prevent — an unknown `profile` surfaces `getTasteProfile`'s error rather than an empty prohibitions block, a `project_dir` with no `.raven/design-system-source.json` falls back to `<project_dir>/DESIGN.md` and reports `design_md_resolved_via:"default"`, an unmatched `project` name yields `binding_resolved:false` plus a `## Gaps` line instead of silent empty notes, and a `decisions_scope` outside `project_dir` is stated in the prompt.
   554	- **On the consultation-trace assertion, and what it actually proves.** A test asserting `consultations.jsonl` is byte-unchanged after a full compose is worth writing — verify the direct-read claim by effect, not by re-reading the call site — but it is **weaker than it looks** and must say so in a comment. `recordConsultation` returns early when `RAVEN_NO_CONSULTATION_TRACE === "1"`, and its write is wrapped in a `catch` that swallows every failure (`src/decision-graph.ts:701-717`), so an unchanged file is also what a traced-but-suppressed run and a traced-but-failed write both look like. The test therefore runs with `RAVEN_NO_CONSULTATION_TRACE` explicitly **unset** and a writable `RAVEN_DECISIONS_HOME`, and proves its point by control: a sibling case calls `decision_list` against the same fixture store and asserts the file **grew**. Byte-unchanged only means something next to a case that changes it. (`test/decision-consultation.test.mjs:180-184` is the existing precedent for driving that env var deliberately.)
   555	- **Falsification gate, and it is not the obvious one.** An earlier draft called `audit_taste`-on-the-prompt *circular* — passing by construction because the prompt injects the same rules the audit grades against. That is wrong, and the truth is worse: it would **fail** by construction. `audit_taste` grades a literal artifact: the engine requires exactly one of `html` or `text` (`src/taste.ts:1298-1303`) — the MCP tool's third input, `url`, renders a page into the same path and is meaningless for a prompt string. It then extracts banned terms from each rule's `negative_prompt` + `clause_text`, and searches the stripped target for them (`src/taste.ts:1846-1852`). A `## Prohibitions` section that correctly quotes *"no glow, no gradient fills, no second accent hue"* verbatim is a target containing the banned terms, so it indicts itself. And the note-fidelity half never runs at all on prompt text: it requires a resolved binding **and** extracted traits (`src/taste.ts:1396-1400`), and arbitrary prose has neither. So `audit_taste` is not a weak gate on a prompt — it is not a gate on a prompt at all. It is a gate on the **built artifact**, which is exactly where §11 already uses it. Phase 0's real gates stand unchanged: (a) Andrew's **blind A/B preference** between two builds of the same component — one from a pasted screenshot, one from this — with the source hidden, and (b) the **count of correction round-trips to acceptance**. Cheaper pre-gate, one hour: compare the composed prompt against a one-line instruction telling the agent to call `read_design_md` + `get_taste_profile` + `audit_taste` itself. If it is no better, delete the tool.
   556	
   557	### Phase 1 — critique session and markup (~1 week)
   558	
   559	Bridge routes `GET /critique`, `GET /reference/<id>.png`, `POST /critique`; write-through persistence to `~/.raven/critique/<id>.json`; re-attachable sessions; `start_grab_session` refuses during `collecting`. Markup tab in `browser/raven-grab.js`, gated on `mode:'critique'`, box/arrow/pin on a plain canvas, typed notes, no dependency. Four session tools. Extraction with the substring quote gate. Rules proposed, never auto-written.
   560	
   561	- **Files:** `browser/raven-grab.js` + `web/public/raven-grab.js` (atomic, plus new `scripts/sync-overlay.mjs` and a byte-equality test), `src/grab-bridge.ts`, new `src/critique.ts`, `src/taste.ts` (additive `ReferenceCapture` fields), `src/index.ts`.
   562	- **Blocking gate before any pixel of that panel is designed:** `get_taste_interview` → `bind_taste_surface` on the panel surface itself, plus a `customer-lens-kickoff`. Raven's own tool description calls the interview a blocking gate; shipping a panel that skips it would be the `bindTasteSurface`-prose-only failure repeating inside Raven.
   563	- **Test:** extend `test/grab-bridge.test.mjs` for the three routes, the key gate, the 409 on a frozen session, and re-attach; a Playwright spec driving the panel on a fixture, skipped when Playwright is absent.
   564	
   565	### Phase 2 — reference persistence and the interaction corpus (~1 week)
   566	
   567	`capture_reference` writes PNGs to `~/.raven/references/` and appends `ReferenceCapture` entries. `search_references` = tag filter + existing `textSearch`, linear scan over the surfaces file. The six interaction pattern files (18 entries) land in `src/data/patterns/`, shipped by the existing `files: ["src/data/"]`.
   568	
   569	- **Files:** new `src/data/patterns/interaction-*.json` (six); `src/index.ts` — two optional fields on `interface Pattern` (`aliases?`, `motion_bearing?`), plus the **three retrieval call sites that must learn to read `aliases` or the field is inert** (§4): `get_pattern`'s fuzzy match (`:2367-2375`), `search_knowledge`'s pattern haystack (`:2583-2588`), and `get_checklist`'s filter (`:2641-2644`); the two new tools with their `TOOL_ACCESS` / `REMOTE_GATED_TOOLS` entries and the tool-count tax those imply (Phase 0's list, `105+n`); `src/taste.ts` (`validateReferences` allowlist branches, §3).
   570	- **Test:** `test/reference-store.test.mjs` (round-trip, quality flags with and without `pngjs`); extend `test/patterns-data.test.mjs` (parse, unique ids, `principles_referenced` resolution, `evidence` URL shape).
   571	
   572	### Phase 3 — motion, and maybe voice (~1 week, gated on demand)
   573	
   574	Only if Phase 1–2 usage shows people asking for motion. Widen the `Interaction` union with a scroll variant; add a `frames` mode to `src/capture.ts` using CDP screencast (scrub as opportunistic enrichment); `MotionSpec.source:"observed"` becomes reachable; A5's **duration and easing** become tool-verifiable at ±16ms (its presence stays the building agent's Playwright assertion — a sampler measures motion, it does not assert a selector). Voice only if long typed notes become a real complaint.
   575	
   576	### Not planned: hosted shared corpus
   577	
   578	See §14. It is listed as a risk, not a phase.
   579	
   580	---
   581	
   582	## 14. Risks
   583	
   584	**The hosted tier does not survive its own constraints — but two reasons an earlier draft gave were false, and removing them matters because they were the load-bearing ones.**
   585	
   586	*False #1: "there is no Playwright on the hosted function, so remote capture is impossible."* There is. `launchAuditChromium()` branches on `isRemoteRuntime()` and the remote branch launches `playwright-core` with `@sparticuz/chromium` (`src/browser-launch.ts:294`, `:314-317`), both real dependencies (`package.json:53,55`), behind a browser-slot semaphore and an egress proxy. Hosted capture is technically available today. It is still ruled out here, on the grounds §5 actually cares about: a third-party capture taken by the hosted function is a third-party capture leaving the user's machine and landing on Raven's infrastructure, which is the one thing §5's first human decision says never happens without a rights-holder takedown path, a named jurisdiction, and a privacy review. Legal and privacy, not capability.
   587	
   588	*False #2: "every store access fails closed under remote."* Only the **anonymous** remote path does. `buildServer` takes an injected `TasteStore` and uses `ClosedTasteStore` only when none is supplied (`src/index.ts:2183-2186`); when one is (the authenticated endpoint's per-user `RedisTasteStore`), the gate re-registers the authenticated taste subset (`src/index.ts:2203-2205`) and the surface is 56 tools, not 45 (`test/taste-remote-full.test.mjs:81,89`). Anonymous remote fails closed; remote-with-store reads and writes real per-user taste data. A hosted corpus story would necessarily run under the **authenticated, store-injected** variant — so "it can't read anything anyway" is not an argument available to it.
   589	
   590	What is left is still decisive: `DESIGN.md` is a local repo file with no remote read path, so the hosted runtime cannot ground a build in the project's own tokens; `api/mcp.js` caps bodies at 400KB, which one screenshot exceeds; and the anonymous surface is hash-frozen at 45 tools. A hosted tier could therefore serve exactly one thing: a shared list of reference records and URLs. That is a smaller Mobbin, which §2 lists as a non-goal, and it does not solve the stated team pain (*"every engineer grounds on a different screenshot"*), because grounding is what it cannot do. Making it real means designing a sync path for project tokens into the hosted runtime — the Redis taste store already namespaces by JWT `sub`, so the pattern exists, but it is a real design task, not a footnote. **Recommendation: do not build it; revisit only if a token-sync design is written first.**

COMPOSED PROMPT RELEVANT
     1	# Build: Snackbar for an optimistic save: confirmation with an inline Undo, auto-dismiss, explicit dismiss
     2	Grounded in: `/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-accunliffe-projects-raven-mcp/597ce6a8-fd03-4b4e-badf-6f00d1dc327e/scratchpad/pregate/arena/DESIGN.md` (resolved via default) · taste `andrew/portfolio editor surface` · inventory DESIGN.md (3 components)
     3	Note: decisions were read from `/Users/accunliffe/projects/raven-mcp/.raven/decisions`, which is outside the project you named.
     4	
     5	## What we're copying (and what we are not)
     6	- [kind: inferred] — auto-dismiss with an inline undo is the established pattern for optimistic saves
     7	We are **not** copying the reference's color, radius, type, or brand surfaces — brand comes only from the tokens below.
     8	
     9	## Structure
    10	- transient confirmation surface, bottom of viewport, single instance [row] → <toast> (no component found in your system — create it or name your equivalent) [emphasis 2 → `type.h3`, density compact → `space.sm`]
    11	  - confirmation message text [stack] → <text> (no component found in your system — create it or name your equivalent) [emphasis 2 → `type.h3`, density compact → `space.sm`]
    12	  - inline undo affordance, text-weight action [stack] → <button> (alias) [emphasis 3 → `type.h2`, density compact → `space.sm`]
    13	  - explicit dismiss affordance [stack] → <button> (alias) [emphasis 1 → `type.body`, density compact → `space.sm`]
    14	
    15	## States
    16	Initial: `hidden`. States: `hidden` — not rendered in the tree; no reserved space · `visible` — entered after an optimistic save commits; auto-dismiss countdown running · `undoing` — undo was chosen; the save is being reverted, snackbar exits immediately
    17	- `hidden` → `visible` on save-committed [source: designer]
    18	- `visible` → `hidden` on timeout 5000ms [source: designer]
    19	- `visible` → `undoing` on undo-pressed [source: designer]
    20	- `visible` → `hidden` on dismiss-pressed [source: designer]
    21	- `undoing` → `hidden` on revert-complete [source: designer]
    22	
    23	## Motion
    24	- snackbar-root on enter: transform, opacity 0→1, 210ms, easing `null` — **source: designer**. 210ms is a designer value, not observed in the reference. Use `motion.duration.base`. Pick the easing from `motion.easing.out-quart` / `motion.easing.out-expo` / `motion.easing.site` and report which.
    25	- `prefers-reduced-motion`: opacity-only.
    26	- snackbar-root on exit: opacity 1→0, 120ms, easing `null` — **source: designer**. 120ms is a designer value, not observed in the reference. Use `motion.duration.fast`. Pick the easing from `motion.easing.out-quart` / `motion.easing.out-expo` / `motion.easing.site` and report which.
    27	- `prefers-reduced-motion`: opacity-only.
    28	
    29	## Content
    30	- message.confirmation: "Change saved" — voice: plain statement, no exclamation, no praise [source: designer]
    31	- undo-action.undo-label: "Undo" — voice: single verb, no ceremony [source: designer]

    46	# arena — andrewcunliffe.ai-derived design brief (pre-gate fixture)
    47	
    48	Dark-first editorial. Ink-on-paper weight, hairline rules (`--line`), one warm
    49	accent (`--accent`), generous negative space. The work is the subject; the
    50	chrome recedes. Restraint over persuasion.
    51	
    52	Fonts (tokens only, never literals): `--font-display`/`--font-body` = Untitled
    53	Sans (400/500/700, no italics loaded — italics are off-limits), `--font-mono` =
    54	Geist, `--font-serif` = Domaine Display SemiBold (authorial voice ONLY — never
    55	titles, headers, metrics, or CTAs).
    56	
    57	Every visual value is `var(--token, fallback)` — no bare hex, px, or font
    58	literals in component CSS. Buttons use semantic classes; every interactive
    59	element has a hover state. Every animation honors `prefers-reduced-motion`.
    60	One accent hue; everything decorative is monochrome.

ARM A LOG
     1	# Build log — snackbar (arm-a)
     2	
     3	Spec: `../composed-prompt.md` · tokens: `../arena/DESIGN.md`
     4	
     5	## Choices I was asked to report
     6	
     7	- **Enter easing:** `motion.easing.out-expo` (`--motion-easing-out-expo`), on both opacity and transform, at `motion.duration.base` (200ms — the spec's 210ms designer value rounds to this token as instructed). Rationale: a confirmation surface should decelerate hard and settle fast; out-expo front-loads the movement so the toast feels immediate without overshoot. `site` is a two-sided ease better suited to on-page layout moves than an off-canvas entry.
     8	- **Exit easing:** `motion.easing.out-quart` (`--motion-easing-out-quart`) at `motion.duration.fast` (120ms), opacity only per spec. A gentler curve than out-expo — an exit should recede, not perform.
     9	- **`prefers-reduced-motion`:** opacity-only (transform: none) AND duration collapsed to `--motion-duration-instant` (0.01ms), satisfying both the Motion section and `MOTION-prefers-reduced-motion`.
    10	
    11	## Gaps resolved (spec §Gaps 1–6)
    12	
    13	1. **No surface binding for "arena":** proceeded with DESIGN.md's brief prose as the taste surface (dark-first editorial, hairline rules, one warm accent, restraint). No interview could be run from this build arm.
    14	2. **Decision-scope mismatch:** noted; the dec_design_01–06 rejections were still honored (line-height ≥ 1.2 everywhere, no nested cards, no orange as general chrome — accent appears only on the Undo action, a functional signal).
    15	3. **"toast" archetype unmatched:** created a new `snackbar` component (`.snackbar`, injected into a persistent `.snackbar-region` live region).
    16	4. **"text" archetype unmatched:** created `.snackbar__message`, a plain `<p>` text element scoped to the component.
    17	5. **button missing `loading` state:** added `.btn.is-loading` (fg-dim, pointer-events none, `aria-busy`) to the semantic button system; applied to the Undo button during `undoing`. Registered here in CSS + this log — no design-system registry is writable from this arm.
    18	6. **button missing `secondary` variant:** substituted the existing **`ghost`** variant for both the inline Undo and the dismiss affordance rather than inventing an unregistered secondary.
    19	
    20	## Other decisions and notes
    21	
    22	- **Emphasis→type mapping applied literally:** message at `type.h3` (27px), Undo at `type.h2` (56px), dismiss at `type.body`. Flagging plainly: a 56px Undo is far larger than any shipping snackbar convention and visually dominates the message; I applied it because the build instruction was to follow the spec exactly and this mapping is not listed as a gap. If this is a compose-time mapping defect, the one-line fix is `type.body` on all three nodes (message medium-weight).
    23	- **Hidden = absent from DOM** (`OTHER-dynamic-dom-not-css-hide`): the snackbar node is created on save-committed and removed after exit; no display:none. A persistent, empty `role="status" aria-live="polite"` region stays in the DOM so injected confirmations actually announce (a live region added at insert time would not).
    24	- **State machine:** implemented exactly as specced (`hidden`/`visible`/`undoing`, all five transitions). "Exits immediately" on undo is read as: exit animation starts at undo-press while the revert runs; `revert-complete` (simulated 400ms) restores the value and lands `hidden`. `data-state` is stamped on the root for testability.
    25	- **Single instance:** a save while visible restarts the 5000ms countdown on the existing snackbar; undo reverts only the latest save (demo model is a history stack).
    26	- **Added semantic tokens** (values not present in DESIGN.md, needed to avoid raw literals in component CSS): `--size-tap-min: 44px`, `--border-hairline: 1px`, `--focus-ring-width: 2px`, `--motion-duration-instant: 0.01ms`, `--motion-distance-enter: var(--space-md)`. All semantic names per `TOKEN-semantic-names`.
    27	- **Fonts:** Untitled Sans / Geist licensed files can't be embedded in this fixture; the `--font-body/--font-display/--font-mono` tokens carry system fallback stacks behind the same names. No font-family literals in component CSS. No serif anywhere (`TYPE-serif-authorial-only`), no italics.
    28	- **Corner radius:** none — no radius token exists in the system and the brief is hairline-ruled editorial; the toast is a sharp-cornered `bg-elev` surface with a `--color-line` hairline border, no shadow (`LAYOUT-no-card-soup`).
    29	- **Contrast check (computed):** accent #ed4609 on bg-elev #141414 ≈ 4.8:1 (AA pass); fg and fg-muted are well clear. Dismiss icon is 20px (allowed set 14/16/20), `stroke="currentColor"`, sized by SVG attributes not CSS.
    30	- **Tap targets:** all three interactive elements carry `min-width/min-height: var(--size-tap-min)` (44px).
    31	- **Hover states:** Save (bg shift), Undo (underline at accent), dismiss (fg-muted → fg). Focus-visible outlines on all buttons.
    32	- **No countdown pause on hover** — the spec's transition table names a plain 5000ms timeout, so no unrequested pause-on-hover behavior was added.
    33	
    34	## A5 — Playwright assertion (agent-asserted criterion)
    35	
    36	```js
    37	await page.click('#save-change');
    38	const bar = page.locator('.snackbar');
    39	await expect(bar).toBeVisible();                    // hidden -> visible on save-committed
    40	await expect(bar).toContainText('Change saved');
    41	await page.click('.snackbar__dismiss');
    42	await expect(bar).toHaveCount(0);                   // visible -> hidden on dismiss-pressed
    43	
    44	await page.click('#save-change');
    45	await expect(page.locator('.snackbar')).toHaveCount(0, { timeout: 6000 }); // 5000ms auto-dismiss
    46	
    47	await page.click('#save-change');                   // value -> Draft N
    48	await page.click('.snackbar__undo');                // visible -> undoing
    49	await expect(page.locator('.snackbar')).toHaveCount(0);      // undoing -> hidden after revert
    50	await expect(page.locator('#demo-value')).not.toHaveText(/Draft 4/); // reverted
    51	```
    52	
    53	Duration/easing remain UNVERIFIED per the spec's own note (no motion sampler); the token wiring is verifiable by reading the CSS.
    54	
    55	## Could not satisfy
    56	
    57	- Gap 1's "run get_taste_interview + bind_taste_surface" — interview is a user-facing gate, not runnable from a build arm; proceeded on DESIGN.md alone as the prompt directs ("do not consult any other design source").
    58	- Real Untitled Sans/Geist rendering (licensed binaries unavailable to a self-contained fixture).

ARM B LOG
     1	# Build log — snackbar (optimistic save), arm-b
     2	
     3	## Tools consulted
     4	
     5	**`read_design_md`** (arena/DESIGN.md) returned the full token index:
     6	- Colors: bg `#050505`, bg-elev `#141414`, bg-card `#1c1c1c`, fg `#ffffff`, fg-muted `#b8b8b8`, fg-dim `#828282`, line `#363636`, line-strong `#545454`, accent `#ed4609` (one warm accent).
     7	- Type scale 13/16/20/27/56/96; space 4/8/16/24/32/48; motion 120/200/400ms with out-quart, out-expo, and site easings.
     8	- Brief: dark-first editorial, hairline rules, one warm accent, generous negative space, restraint over persuasion. Untitled Sans body, Geist mono, serif is authorial-voice only. Every visual value `var(--token, fallback)`; semantic button classes; hover states on every interactive element; `prefers-reduced-motion` honored; everything decorative monochrome.
     9	
    10	**`get_taste_profile` ("andrew")** — 37-rule catalog. The rules that shaped this component: `COLOR-accent-punctuation-not-fill`, `COLOR-no-gradient-no-glow`, `TOKEN-no-bare-literals`, `TOKEN-semantic-button-classes`, `OTHER-hover-state-required`, `SPACING-tap-targets-44px`, `MOTION-prefers-reduced-motion`, `OTHER-dynamic-dom-not-css-hide`, `ASSET-icon-stroke-current-color`, `TYPE-serif-authorial-only`, `VOICE-editorial-restraint`, `LAYOUT-no-bare-modals`.
    11	
    12	## Choices
    13	
    14	- **Accent as punctuation, not fill:** the Undo action is accent-colored *text* on the elevated surface — the only accent on the page. The confirmation surface itself is monochrome (`bg-elev` + 1px `line` hairline border). No gradient, no glow, no drop shadow — elevation is carried by the hairline and the lighter surface, which fits the ink-on-paper brief.
    15	- **Placement:** fixed bottom-left with `--space-xl` margins — editorial rather than the centered-toast default (`OTHER-no-shadcn-defaults`).
    16	- **Behavior:** optimistic — the setting value flips immediately on Save; the snackbar confirms with inline **Undo** (reverts to the captured previous value), an explicit **dismiss** (X, `currentColor` stroke icon), and an 8s auto-dismiss. The timer pauses on hover and on keyboard focus within the bar and resumes with remaining time, so Undo can't vanish under the user's cursor. A new save replaces the current snackbar rather than stacking.
    17	- **A11y:** persistent `role="status" aria-live="polite"` region (the live region exists before content is injected, so announcements fire reliably); both actions are real `<button>`s with `min-height/min-width: 44px`; dismiss has an `aria-label`; visible `:focus-visible` outline; `prefers-reduced-motion` disables transitions in CSS *and* skips the JS exit animation.
    18	- **Dynamic DOM, not CSS hide:** the snackbar is created on save and removed from the DOM after the exit transition (with a timeout fallback in case `transitionend` never fires), per `OTHER-dynamic-dom-not-css-hide`.
    19	- **Tokens:** all DESIGN.md values defined as `:root` custom properties; every component declaration is `var(--token, fallback)` — no bare hex/px in component CSS. Buttons are semantic classes (`.btn`, `.btn-primary`, `.btn-undo`, `.btn-dismiss`) with hover, active, focus-visible, and disabled styling.
    20	- **Fonts:** `--font-body` names Untitled Sans with a Helvetica fallback stack (licensed woff2 files are not available in this fixture to inline); no italics, weights limited to 400/500/700; no serif anywhere (nothing authorial on this surface).
    21	- **Demo harness:** one hairline-bounded setting row ("Project visibility" Private/Public) plus a single "Save change" primary button, so save → undo produces a visibly verifiable state change. Copy is plain and concrete, no persuasion.
    22	
    23	## audit_taste result
    24	
    25	`audit_taste` (profile "andrew", surface "component-demo", html mode): **Verdict: PASS — no findings, none suppressed.** All deterministic detectors clean (no gradients, no glow shadows, no second accent hue, no banned words, no bare literals). 29 judgment-only rules reported honestly as `not_assessed` (no deterministic detector); each of the applicable ones was addressed by construction as listed above. `COLOR-one-warm-orange-accent` was skipped as out-of-scope for this surface (portfolio-monochrome scope) — the build honors it anyway with `#ed4609` as the sole accent.
    26

    28	
    29	- Untitled Sans/Geist are referenced by token with system fallbacks rather than embedded, since the font files are not present in the fixture directory and the artifact must make no external requests.

   265	  <p class="demo__kicker">Demo — optimistic save</p>
   266	  <div class="demo__row">
   267	    <span class="demo__label">Project title</span>

   273	<!-- Persistent polite live region; the snackbar is injected here and is
   274	     absent from the DOM entirely while hidden. -->
   275	<div class="snackbar-region" role="status" aria-live="polite"></div>
   276	
   277	<script>
   278	  (function () {
   279	    "use strict";
   280	
   281	    var AUTO_DISMISS_MS = 5000; // designer value from the States table
   282	    var REVERT_MS = 400;        // simulated revert latency (demo harness)
   283	    var EXIT_FALLBACK_MS = 400; // safety net if transitionend never fires
   284	
   285	    var region = document.querySelector(".snackbar-region");
   286	    var saveBtn = document.getElementById("save-change");
   287	    var valueEl = document.getElementById("demo-value");
   288	
   289	    // Demo model: a stack of saved values; undo pops the latest save.
   290	    var history = ["Draft 1"];
   291	    var revision = 1;
   292	
   293	    // Component state machine: hidden | visible | undoing
   294	    var state = "hidden";
   295	    var snackbarEl = null;
   296	    var dismissTimer = null;
   297	
   298	    function render() {
   299	      valueEl.textContent = history[history.length - 1];
   300	    }
   301	
   302	    function clearDismissTimer() {
   303	      if (dismissTimer !== null) {
   304	        clearTimeout(dismissTimer);
   305	        dismissTimer = null;
   306	      }
   307	    }
   308	
   309	    function startDismissTimer() {
   310	      clearDismissTimer();
   311	      dismissTimer = setTimeout(function () {
   312	        // visible -> hidden on timeout 5000ms
   313	        if (state === "visible") {
   314	          state = "hidden";
   315	          beginExit();
   316	        }
   317	      }, AUTO_DISMISS_MS);
   318	    }
   319	
   320	    function buildSnackbar() {
   321	      var el = document.createElement("div");
   322	      el.className = "snackbar";
   323	      el.dataset.state = "visible";
   324	
   325	      var msg = document.createElement("p");
   326	      msg.className = "snackbar__message";
   327	      msg.textContent = "Change saved";
   328	
   329	      var undo = document.createElement("button");
   330	      undo.type = "button";
   331	      undo.className = "btn btn--ghost snackbar__undo";
   332	      undo.textContent = "Undo";
   333	      undo.addEventListener("click", onUndoPressed);
   334	
   335	      var dismiss = document.createElement("button");
   336	      dismiss.type = "button";
   337	      dismiss.className = "btn btn--ghost snackbar__dismiss";
   338	      dismiss.setAttribute("aria-label", "Dismiss");
   339	      dismiss.innerHTML =
   340	        '<svg width="20" height="20" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" ' +
   341	        'stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" ' +
   342	        'aria-hidden="true"><path d="M6 6l12 12M18 6L6 18"/></svg>';
   343	      dismiss.addEventListener("click", onDismissPressed);
   344	
   345	      el.appendChild(msg);
   346	      el.appendChild(undo);
   347	      el.appendChild(dismiss);
   348	      return el;
   349	    }
   350	
   351	    function show() {
   352	      if (state === "visible" && snackbarEl) {
   353	        // single instance: a new save while visible restarts the countdown
   354	        startDismissTimer();
   355	        return;
   356	      }
   357	      if (state === "undoing") return; // save is disabled during undoing anyway
   358	
   359	      snackbarEl = buildSnackbar();
   360	      region.appendChild(snackbarEl);
   361	      state = "visible";
   362	
   363	      // double rAF so the enter transition reliably plays from its start values
   364	      requestAnimationFrame(function () {
   365	        requestAnimationFrame(function () {
   366	          if (snackbarEl) snackbarEl.classList.add("is-visible");
   367	        });
   368	      });
   369	      startDismissTimer();
   370	    }
   371	
   372	    function beginExit() {
   373	      var node = snackbarEl;
   374	      if (!node) return;
   375	      snackbarEl = null;
   376	      var removed = false;
   377	
   378	      function remove() {
   379	        if (removed) return;
   380	        removed = true;
   381	        if (node.contains(document.activeElement)) saveBtn.focus();
   382	        node.remove();
   383	      }
   384	
   385	      node.classList.add("is-exiting");
   386	      node.addEventListener("transitionend", function (e) {
   387	        if (e.target === node && e.propertyName === "opacity") remove();
   388	      });
   389	      setTimeout(remove, EXIT_FALLBACK_MS);
   390	    }
   391	
   392	    function onDismissPressed() {
   393	      // visible -> hidden on dismiss-pressed (the save stands)
   394	      if (state !== "visible") return;
   395	      clearDismissTimer();
   396	      state = "hidden";
   397	      beginExit();
   398	    }
   399	
   400	    function onUndoPressed() {
   401	      // visible -> undoing on undo-pressed
   402	      if (state !== "visible") return;
   403	      clearDismissTimer();
   404	      state = "undoing";
   405	
   406	      var node = snackbarEl;
   407	      if (node) {
   408	        node.dataset.state = "undoing";
   409	        var buttons = node.querySelectorAll("button");
   410	        for (var i = 0; i < buttons.length; i++) buttons[i].disabled = true;
   411	        var undoBtn = node.querySelector(".snackbar__undo");
   412	        if (undoBtn) {
   413	          undoBtn.classList.add("is-loading");
   414	          undoBtn.setAttribute("aria-busy", "true");
   415	        }
   416	      }
   417	      saveBtn.disabled = true;
   418	
   419	      // the snackbar exits immediately while the revert runs
   420	      beginExit();
   421	
   422	      // undoing -> hidden on revert-complete
   423	      setTimeout(function () {
   424	        if (history.length > 1) history.pop();
   425	        render();
   426	        state = "hidden";
   427	        saveBtn.disabled = false;
   428	      }, REVERT_MS);
   429	    }
   430	
   431	    saveBtn.addEventListener("click", function () {
   432	      // optimistic save: apply the change first, then confirm
   433	      revision += 1;
   434	      history.push("Draft " + revision);
   435	      render();
   436	      show(); // hidden -> visible on save-committed
   437	    });
   438	
   439	    render();
   440	  })();

   201	    flex-shrink: 0;
   202	  }
   203	
   204	  .btn-undo:hover,
   205	  .btn-undo:active { color: var(--color-fg, #ffffff); }
   206

   228	
   229	<main class="demo">
   230	  <p class="demo-eyebrow">Component demo</p>
   231	  <h1>Optimistic save</h1>
   232	  <p class="demo-note">Saving applies the change immediately. The snackbar
   233	    confirms it, offers Undo, and dismisses itself after eight seconds — or
   234	    sooner, if you dismiss it.</p>
   235	
   236	  <div class="setting">
   237	    <div>
   238	      <p class="setting-label">Project visibility</p>
   239	      <p class="setting-value" id="setting-value">Private</p>
   240	    </div>
   241	    <button class="btn btn-primary" id="save-btn" type="button">Save change</button>
   242	  </div>
   243	</main>
   244	
   245	<div class="snackbar-region" id="snackbar-region" role="status" aria-live="polite"></div>
   246	
   247	<script>
   248	  (function () {
   249	    var AUTO_DISMISS_MS = 8000;
   250	    var STATES = ['Private', 'Public'];
   251	
   252	    var valueEl = document.getElementById('setting-value');
   253	    var saveBtn = document.getElementById('save-btn');
   254	    var region = document.getElementById('snackbar-region');
   255	
   256	    var reducedMotion = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)');
   257	    var current = null; // { el, timer, deadline, remaining, prevValue }
   258	
   259	    function nextValue(v) {
   260	      return STATES[(STATES.indexOf(v) + 1) % STATES.length];
   261	    }
   262	
   263	    function buildSnackbar(newVal, prevVal) {
   264	      var bar = document.createElement('div');
   265	      bar.className = 'snackbar is-entering';
   266	
   267	      var text = document.createElement('p');
   268	      text.className = 'snackbar-text';
   269	      text.innerHTML = 'Visibility set to <strong>' + newVal + '</strong>';
   270	      bar.appendChild(text);
   271	
   272	      var undo = document.createElement('button');
   273	      undo.type = 'button';
   274	      undo.className = 'btn btn-undo';
   275	      undo.textContent = 'Undo';
   276	      undo.addEventListener('click', function () {
   277	        valueEl.textContent = prevVal;
   278	        dismiss(bar);
   279	      });
   280	      bar.appendChild(undo);
   281	
   282	      var close = document.createElement('button');
   283	      close.type = 'button';
   284	      close.className = 'btn btn-dismiss';
   285	      close.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Dismiss notification');
   286	      close.innerHTML =
   287	        '<svg viewBox="0 0 16 16" fill="none" stroke-width="1.5" aria-hidden="true">' +
   288	        '<path d="M3 3l10 10M13 3L3 13"/></svg>';
   289	      close.addEventListener('click', function () { dismiss(bar); });
   290	      bar.appendChild(close);
   291	
   292	      // Pause the auto-dismiss clock while the user is engaged with it.
   293	      bar.addEventListener('mouseenter', pauseTimer);
   294	      bar.addEventListener('mouseleave', resumeTimer);
   295	      bar.addEventListener('focusin', pauseTimer);
   296	      bar.addEventListener('focusout', function (e) {
   297	        if (!bar.contains(e.relatedTarget)) resumeTimer();
   298	      });
   299	
   300	      return bar;
   301	    }
   302	
   303	    function show(newVal, prevVal) {
   304	      // One snackbar at a time: the newest save replaces the last, DOM-removed.
   305	      if (current) removeNow(current.el);
   306	
   307	      var bar = buildSnackbar(newVal, prevVal);
   308	      region.appendChild(bar);
   309	
   310	      // enter transition
   311	      requestAnimationFrame(function () {
   312	        requestAnimationFrame(function () {
   313	          bar.classList.remove('is-entering');
   314	        });
   315	      });
   316	
   317	      current = {
   318	        el: bar,
   319	        timer: null,
   320	        remaining: AUTO_DISMISS_MS,
   321	        startedAt: null
   322	      };
   323	      startTimer();
   324	    }
   325	
   326	    function startTimer() {
   327	      if (!current) return;
   328	      current.startedAt = Date.now();
   329	      current.timer = setTimeout(function () {
   330	        if (current) dismiss(current.el);
   331	      }, current.remaining);
   332	    }
   333	
   334	    function pauseTimer() {
   335	      if (!current || current.timer === null) return;
   336	      clearTimeout(current.timer);
   337	      current.timer = null;
   338	      current.remaining -= Date.now() - current.startedAt;
   339	      if (current.remaining < 0) current.remaining = 0;
   340	    }
   341	
   342	    function resumeTimer() {
   343	      if (!current || current.timer !== null) return;
   344	      startTimer();
   345	    }
   346	
   347	    function removeNow(el) {
   348	      if (current && current.el === el) {
   349	        if (current.timer !== null) clearTimeout(current.timer);
   350	        current = null;
   351	      }
   352	      if (el.parentNode) el.parentNode.removeChild(el);
   353	    }
   354	
   355	    function dismiss(el) {
   356	      if (current && current.el === el) {
   357	        if (current.timer !== null) clearTimeout(current.timer);
   358	        current = null;
   359	      }
   360	      if (reducedMotion.matches) {
   361	        removeNow(el);
   362	        return;
   363	      }
   364	      el.classList.add('is-leaving');
   365	      var done = false;
   366	      el.addEventListener('transitionend', function () {
   367	        if (!done) { done = true; removeNow(el); }
   368	      });
   369	      // Fallback in case transitionend never fires (e.g. tab hidden).
   370	      setTimeout(function () {
   371	        if (!done) { done = true; removeNow(el); }
   372	      }, 600);
   373	    }
   374	
   375	    saveBtn.addEventListener('click', function () {
   376	      var prev = valueEl.textContent;
   377	      var next = nextValue(prev);
   378	      // Optimistic: apply immediately, then confirm.
   379	      valueEl.textContent = next;
   380	      show(next, prev);
   381	    });
   382	  })();
   383	</script>
   384	
   385	</body>

SOURCE MAPPING
   300	  return tokens.filter(function(token) {
   301	    return token.group === prefix || token.path === prefix || token.path.indexOf(prefix + ".") === 0;
   302	  });
   303	}
   304	
   305	// Sort the group's parseable scalars ascending and pick by quantile — never by
   306	// declaration order, which carries no semantics in a YAML block (§9). Fewer
   307	// than two parseable values is not a ramp: unresolved, reported as a gap.
   308	function rampPick(tokens: FlattenedDesignToken[], prefix: string, quantile: number): string | null {
   309	  var ramp = tokensInGroup(tokens, prefix)
   310	    .map(function(token) { return { token: token, num: parseTokenNumber(token) }; })
   311	    .filter(function(entry): entry is { token: FlattenedDesignToken; num: number } { return entry.num !== null; })
   312	    .sort(function(a, b) { return a.num - b.num; });
   313	  if (ramp.length < 2) return null;
   314	  var index = Math.min(ramp.length - 1, Math.max(0, Math.round((ramp.length - 1) * quantile)));
   315	  return ramp[index].token.path;
   316	}
   317	
   318	var EMPHASIS_QUANTILE: Record<number, number> = { 1: 0.25, 2: 0.5, 3: 0.85 };
   319	var DENSITY_QUANTILE: Record<string, number> = { compact: 0.25, default: 0.5, roomy: 0.75 };
   320	
   321	interface CanonicalComponent { id: string; aliases: string[]; states: string[]; variants: string[]; }
   322	
   323	function bindArchetype(archetype: string, components: PromptComponent[], canonical: CanonicalComponent[]): { component: string | null; confidence: SkeletonComponentBinding["confidence"] } {
   324	  var lower = archetype.toLowerCase();
   325	  var direct = components.find(function(comp) {
   326	    return comp.id.toLowerCase() === lower || comp.aliases.some(function(alias) { return alias.toLowerCase() === lower; });
   327	  });
   328	  if (direct) return { component: direct.id, confidence: "alias" };
   329	
   330	  var canon = canonical.find(function(entry) {
   331	    return entry.id === lower || entry.aliases.indexOf(lower) !== -1;
   332	  });
   333	  if (canon) {
   334	    var names = [canon.id].concat(canon.aliases);
   335	    var viaCanon = components.find(function(comp) {
   336	      var compNames = [comp.id.toLowerCase()].concat(comp.aliases.map(function(alias) { return alias.toLowerCase(); }));
   337	      return compNames.some(function(name) { return names.indexOf(name) !== -1; });
   338	    });
   339	    if (viaCanon) return { component: viaCanon.id, confidence: "canonical" };
   340	  }
   341	
   342	  var best: { id: string; score: number } | null = null;
   343	  for (var i = 0; i < components.length; i++) {
   344	    var score = normalizedLevenshtein(lower, components[i].id.toLowerCase());
   345	    if (!best || score > best.score) best = { id: components[i].id, score: score };
   346	  }
   347	  if (best && best.score >= 0.8) return { component: best.id, confidence: "fuzzy" };
   348	  return { component: null, confidence: "unresolved" };
   349	}
   350	
   351	export function bindSkeleton(
   352	  skeleton: Skeleton,
   353	  components: PromptComponent[],
   354	  tokens: FlattenedDesignToken[],
   355	  canonical: CanonicalComponent[]
   356	): { bindings: SkeletonBinding[]; gaps: string[] } {
   357	  var bindings: SkeletonBinding[] = [];
   358	  var gaps: string[] = [];
   359	  var emphasisGapReported = false;
   360	  var densityGapReported = false;
   361	
   362	  walkStructure(skeleton.structure, function(node) {
   363	    var bound = bindArchetype(node.archetype, components, canonical);
   364	    if (bound.confidence === "unresolved" && components.length > 0) {
   365	      gaps.push("Archetype \"" + node.archetype + "\" (node " + node.node_id + ") matched no component in your system — name your equivalent or create one, and report which.");
   366	    }
   367	    var emphasisToken = rampPick(tokens, "type", EMPHASIS_QUANTILE[node.emphasis]);
   368	    if (emphasisToken === null && !emphasisGapReported) {
   369	      gaps.push("type.* has no parseable numeric ramp, so emphasis could not be bound to a token. Name the type token you used for each emphasis level.");
   370	      emphasisGapReported = true;
   371	    }
   372	    var densityToken = rampPick(tokens, "space", DENSITY_QUANTILE[node.density]);
   373	    if (densityToken === null && !densityGapReported) {
   374	      gaps.push("space.* has no parseable numeric ramp, so density could not be bound to a token. Name the spacing token you used.");
   375	      densityGapReported = true;
   376	    }
   377	    bindings.push({
   378	      kind: "component",
   379	      node_id: node.node_id,
   380	      archetype: node.archetype,

319-interface StateMachine {
--
361-### Binding
362-
363-`bindSkeleton(skeleton, inventory, tokens, taste)` is deterministic, not a model call:
364-
365-1. `archetype` → the user's component via `InventoryComponent.aliases`, then the `raven-canonical` alias table, then **normalized Levenshtein ≥ 0.8 on lowercased ids**. The metric is named because an unnamed "string similarity ≥ 0.8" is untunable and unverifiable; the threshold is validated by measuring it against the canonical component list rather than by reading the constant. Emits `confidence: "alias"|"canonical"|"fuzzy"|"unresolved"`.
366:2. `emphasis`/`density` → token paths **by parsing the numeric value of each token in the group and sorting the ramp**, never by declaration order. Declaration order in a YAML block carries no semantics: `type.body.sm`, `type.display.lg`, `type.mono.xs` can appear in any sequence, and ranking by it is a coin flip in the one step where the whole colorless-skeleton claim cashes out. Where a group has no parseable numeric ramp, or a `ref` chain makes it ambiguous, the binding is **unresolved and goes to Gaps**.
367-3. `MotionSpec` numbers → the nearest `motion.duration.*` / `motion.easing.*` token within ±40ms; otherwise the literal is kept and flagged `motion_token_gap`.
368-4. Anything unresolved is **never guessed** — it becomes a named decision in `## Gaps` that the building agent must make and report.

We are **not** copying either app's color, radius, type, or the accent-tinted surface at `ref_8812` box `[0.03,0.86,0.49,0.07]` [kind: pixel] — Andrew flagged that as their brand.

433-> ```

435:> ├─ label kind=body   → Snackbar.Message                    [emphasis 2]
436:> └─ action            → <Button variant="ghost" size="sm">  [emphasis 1]
437-> ```
438-> Anchor bottom-start, offset `space.4`. One instance at a time; a second save replaces the message and restarts the timer.

441-> `idle` → `committed` on save · `committed` → `confirmed` on ack · `committed|confirmed` → `reverted` on undo · `committed` → `failed` on error · `confirmed` → `idle` on timeout 6000ms, paused on hover and focus-within · `failed` has no timeout.

536-
537-The reference is named by `reference_url` or by an existing bound `ReferenceCapture`, is used for provenance, and is **never fetched** by the composer. **It is not compared with `referenceDeltas()`** — that function takes the *built* target's `PageTraits` (`src/taste-fidelity.ts:448`) and Phase 0 has no built target at all: no HTML, no URL, no screenshot, no traits. It belongs to §11's post-build verification. Where compose-time intent needs checking against a reference, the function whose actual job that is, is `checkBindingConsistency(design_notes, references)` (`src/taste.ts:1169`) — already what `bind_taste_surface` calls (`src/index.ts:7389`).
538-
539-`project_dir` roots the file reads and (via `basename`) the binding lookup; it does not scope the Decision Graph, whose store location is a module global. That named limitation and its upgrade path are in §9. No corpus, no UI, no new dependency.
540-
541:**It does not derive the skeleton, and that is the point of the small estimate.** Deriving a Structure/States tree from a reference needs a model looking at the reference; Raven has neither a model nor the reference's pixels — a bound `ReferenceCapture` is a URL, a prose `liked` line, aggregate `PageTraits`, and a timestamp (`src/taste.ts:92`), and `PageTraits` is page-level aggregate metrics (`src/capture.ts:285-303`), not a node tree. The agent calling the tool is already looking at the reference. So: `skeleton` is an optional caller-supplied argument. With one, the composer lints it (colorless/typeless/sizeless), binds archetypes to the project's real components and `emphasis`/`density` to real token ramps, and emits the full template. Without one, it emits the grounding half — Tokens, Prohibitions, Gaps, Acceptance criteria — sets `skeleton_required: true`, and instructs the agent to derive Structure/States from the reference and call again. Anything else would mean adding an LLM dependency to a server that has deliberately never had one (`ingest_transcript` is the standing precedent).
542-
543-- **Files, and the list is longer than the code.** New `src/reference-prompt.ts` — the composer **and** the `Skeleton` / `StructureNode` / `StateMachine` / `ContentSlot` / `MotionSpec` / `Provenance` types (§9); no separate `src/pattern-skeleton.ts`. `src/index.ts`: one `server.tool`, one `TOOL_ACCESS` entry (`toolAnnotations()` throws `"Missing MCP tool classification"` without it, §12), one `REMOTE_GATED_TOOLS` entry — **and the three count comments those constants are documented by**, because the tool counts are a frozen contract, not commentary.
544-- **The tool count is a frozen contract, and one gated tool moves two of the three numbers.** Registering `compose_build_prompt` as remote-gated takes the local surface **105 → 106** and the gated set **60 → 61**. The anonymous remote surface stays at **45** and the `GOLDEN_45_HASH` is untouched — which is the whole reason it is gated (§6). Source constants to update: the gated/remote/local comment block at `src/index.ts:1851-1857` ("60 gated tools", "45 stateless", "from 105 local tools"), the `buildServer` header comment at `:2159-2170` ("all 105 local tools", "serve only the 45 stateless remote-safe tools", "gate off the 60 gated tools", "all 105"), and `main()`'s comment at `:7768-7771` ("serves all 105 tools"). Six existing tests assert these and all six must move in the same change:
545-  - `test/taste-remote-full.test.mjs:81-93` — test *name* embeds "stdio = 105"; `:93` asserts `105`.

   350	
   351	interface Provenance {
   352	  claim: string;
   353	  kind: "pixel" | "note" | "pattern" | "inferred";   // required
   354	  ref_id?: string; bbox?: [number,number,number,number];
   355	  pattern_ref?: string;
   356	}
   357	```
   358	
   359	**`source` and `kind` are the fix for the biggest failure this spec could ship.** A static screenshot cannot show a duration, an easing curve, the absence of a state, or a 6000ms timeout. An earlier draft asserted `cubic-bezier(0.2,0,0,1)` "measured from `ref_8812`" when nothing in Raven could have measured it. Rules now enforced by the composer: `easing` is `null` unless a Phase 3 motion capture produced it; a `StateMachine` transition or `ContentSlot` may never carry `kind:"pixel"` — a screenshot cannot show a transition or a copy rule, so that value is excluded from their `kind` unions at the type level rather than checked in prose; and every non-pixel behavioral line in the emitted prompt is prefixed with its source ("180ms is a pattern default, not observed in ref_8812").
   360	
   361	### Binding
   362	
   363	`bindSkeleton(skeleton, inventory, tokens, taste)` is deterministic, not a model call:
   364	
   365	1. `archetype` → the user's component via `InventoryComponent.aliases`, then the `raven-canonical` alias table, then **normalized Levenshtein ≥ 0.8 on lowercased ids**. The metric is named because an unnamed "string similarity ≥ 0.8" is untunable and unverifiable; the threshold is validated by measuring it against the canonical component list rather than by reading the constant. Emits `confidence: "alias"|"canonical"|"fuzzy"|"unresolved"`.
   366	2. `emphasis`/`density` → token paths **by parsing the numeric value of each token in the group and sorting the ramp**, never by declaration order. Declaration order in a YAML block carries no semantics: `type.body.sm`, `type.display.lg`, `type.mono.xs` can appear in any sequence, and ranking by it is a coin flip in the one step where the whole colorless-skeleton claim cashes out. Where a group has no parseable numeric ramp, or a `ref` chain makes it ambiguous, the binding is **unresolved and goes to Gaps**.
   367	3. `MotionSpec` numbers → the nearest `motion.duration.*` / `motion.easing.*` token within ±40ms; otherwise the literal is kept and flagged `motion_token_gap`.
   368	4. Anything unresolved is **never guessed** — it becomes a named decision in `## Gaps` that the building agent must make and report.
   369	
   370	### Inventory ladder
   371	
   372	1. **DESIGN.md component manifest.** Authoritative, already wired. When empty, keep `inventory_design_system`'s existing phrasing — *"no component declarations found — component coverage is unknown, not missing"* (`src/design-system-diff.ts:49`) — which is the correct epistemics.
   373	2. **shadcn `registry.json`** at the project root → `ComponentDecl`s with `evidence:"registry"`. ~30 lines.
   374	3. **Repo scan** — glob `src/components/**/*.{tsx,jsx,vue,svelte}`, exported PascalCase symbols, `evidence:"filename"`. Names only. Ceiling: cannot tell whether `Toast` supports an action slot.
   375	4. **None** → `unbound` mode. Archetypes stay as canonical ids, every component reads `<Snackbar> (no component found in your system — create it or name your equivalent)`, and the first prompt section asks the agent to run `configure_design_system_source` and re-synthesize. It never silently emits shadcn imports.
   376	
   377	**Dropped: Storybook and Figma rungs.** Storybook requires an HTTP fetch to a caller origin, which would make `compose_build_prompt` open-world and force `TOOL_OPEN_WORLD` + `REMOTE_URL_GUARDED_TOOLS` membership; if wanted later, it takes a pre-fetched `index.json` blob as an argument and stays closed-world. Figma is not possible at all: `get_variable_defs` and `get_code_connect_map` are Figma MCP tools, and Raven is an MCP server — it cannot call another MCP server. If Figma enrichment is wanted, the calling agent fetches it and passes it in as an optional `components[]` argument.
   378	
   379	**Dropped: `fallback_system`.** Grounding in `linear.json` when the project has no DESIGN.md is the exact failure this feature exists to prevent. Refuse and point at `init_design_md`.
   380	
   381	### Output template
   382	
   383	Fixed section order, always:
   384	
   385	```
   386	# Build: <intent>
   387	Grounded in: <DESIGN.md path> · taste <profile>/<surface> · inventory <source> (<n> components)
   388	Reference: <ref_id> (<app>, captured <date>) [+ n more]
   389	
   390	## What we're copying (and what we are not)
   391	## Structure            <- bound tree, real component names
   392	## States               <- state machine, each row tagged with its source
   393	## Motion               <- numeric, each spec tagged observed/pattern/designer/default
   394	## Content              <- slots with real copy + voice constraint
   395	## Tokens to use        <- explicit token names, no literals
   396	## Prohibitions         <- negative_prompts + rejected alternatives, verbatim
   397	## Gaps / decisions for you
   398	## Acceptance criteria
   399	```
   400	
   401	---

   403	## 10. The worked example
   404	
   405	Andrew, in Claude Code, in `andrewcunliffe-portfolio`. Target: an optimistic-save affordance on the deck's settings surface. Project declares `Button`, `Snackbar`, `IconButton` in DESIGN.md; tokens under `color.*`, `space.*`, `motion.*`.
   406	
   407	**1.** Andrew: *"I want the save on this to work like Linear's — commits immediately, undo in the confirmation. Let me show you."*
   408	
   409	**2.** Claude → `get_taste_profile({name:"andrew"})` → rules including `TASTE-004 negative_prompt: "no glow, no gradient fills, no second accent hue"` and `TASTE-011: "no spring or bounce easing on functional feedback"`.
   410	
   411	**3.** Claude → `start_critique_session({project:"andrewcunliffe-portfolio", surface:"deck", brief:"optimistic save with undo", profile:"andrew"})` → `{session_id:"crq_8f21", panel_url:"http://127.0.0.1:52814/critique?key=…"}`.
   412	
   413	**4.** Claude → `capture_reference({session_id:"crq_8f21", url:"https://linear.app/settings", app:"Linear", owner:"third-party"})` → `{ref_id:"ref_8812", image_path:"~/.raven/references/ref_8812.png", traits:{…}}`. Same for `ref_9034` (Things 3, a local screenshot Andrew already had).
   414	
   415	**5.** Claude: *"Two references in the panel at that URL. Box what you mean and type a line under each."*
   416	
   417	**6.** Andrew boxes the status strip on `ref_8812` at `[0.03,0.86,0.49,0.07]`, notes: *"the undo lives inside the confirmation, not a separate toast."* Boxes `ref_9034`'s action label, notes: *"text button, not an icon."* Pins the accent-tinted surface on `ref_8812`, notes: *"not this — that's their brand."*
   418	
   419	**7.** Claude → `get_critique_session({session_id:"crq_8f21", since_seq:0, timeout_ms:120000})` → 3 annotations, `state:"collecting"`.
   420	
   421	**8.** Claude → `close_critique_session({mode:"extract"})` → `{extraction_prompt}`, answers it itself, then `record_critique_results({session_id, extraction_json, write:["references","taste_decision_drafts"]})` → `{references_written:2, drafts:1, rejected:[]}`. The one rejection would have been an attribute whose quote was not a substring of any stored note.
   422	
   423	**9.** Claude writes the Structure/States skeleton itself — it has both references and all three notes in context — and passes it in: `compose_build_prompt({intent:"optimistic save with an undo affordance", project_dir:"~/projects/andrewcunliffe-portfolio", profile:"andrew", skeleton:{…}, session_id:"crq_8f21", surface:"deck"})`. `project_dir` roots the DESIGN.md read and the inventory, and its basename `andrewcunliffe-portfolio` is what resolves the surface binding — the same string step 3 bound as `project`. It does **not** scope the Decision Graph: `dec_2291` below was read from the store `decisionsHome()` resolved from the server's own cwd, which the emitted `grounding.decisions_scope` states (§9). Had it called without `skeleton`, the return would have been the grounding block plus the instruction to derive one and re-submit. Emitted prompt, abridged:
   424	
   425	> **# Build: optimistic save with an undo affordance**
   426	> Grounded in: `./DESIGN.md` · taste `andrew/deck` · inventory `DESIGN.md` (14 components)
   427	> Reference: `ref_8812` (Linear, captured 2026-08-01) + `ref_9034` (Things 3)
   428	>
   429	> **## What we're copying (and what we are not)**
   430	> From `ref_8812` [kind: note] — the undo lives inside the confirmation, not a separate toast stack. From `ref_9034` [kind: note] — the undo is a text button, not an icon. We are **not** copying either app's color, radius, type, or the accent-tinted surface at `ref_8812` box `[0.03,0.86,0.49,0.07]` [kind: pixel] — Andrew flagged that as their brand.
   431	>
   432	> **## Structure**
   433	> ```
   434	> region role=status   → <Snackbar variant="neutral">        [emphasis 2, density compact]
   435	> ├─ label kind=body   → Snackbar.Message                    [emphasis 2]
   436	> └─ action            → <Button variant="ghost" size="sm">  [emphasis 1]
   437	> ```
   438	> Anchor bottom-start, offset `space.4`. One instance at a time; a second save replaces the message and restarts the timer.
   439	>
   440	> **## States** (source: `get_pattern("interaction-feedback-status" → undo-snackbar)`, not observed)
   441	> `idle` → `committed` on save · `committed` → `confirmed` on ack · `committed|confirmed` → `reverted` on undo · `committed` → `failed` on error · `confirmed` → `idle` on timeout 6000ms, paused on hover and focus-within · `failed` has no timeout.
   442	>
   443	> **## Motion**
   444	> enter: `opacity 0→1`, `translateY 8→0`, `180ms`, easing `null` — **source: default**. Use `motion.duration.fast` + `motion.easing.entrance`. Nothing in the references was motion-captured; do not treat 180ms as observed.
   445	> `prefers-reduced-motion`: opacity-only, same duration.
   446	>
   447	> **## Tokens to use**
   448	> `color.surface.raised`, `color.text.primary`, `color.border.subtle`, `color.accent.default` (action label only), `space.3`, `space.4`, `radius.md`, `elevation.1`, `type.body.sm`, `motion.duration.fast`, `motion.easing.entrance`. No hex, no px, no font-family literals.
   449	>
   450	> **## Prohibitions** (verbatim)
   451	> `TASTE-004`: "no glow, no gradient fills, no second accent hue" · `TASTE-011`: "no spring or bounce easing on functional feedback" · rejected in the Decision Graph (`dec_2291`, active — read via `listActiveDecisions()`, not `decision_list`): center-screen toasts — "obscures the content the user just edited".
   452	>
   453	> **## Gaps / decisions for you**
   454	> 1. `Snackbar` has no `error` variant (`diff_design_system`: missing state `error`). Add it and register it, or use `variant="neutral"` with a swapped message. Report which.
   455	> 2. `type.*` has no parseable numeric ramp (values are `ref` chains), so `emphasis` could not be bound. Name the token you used for the message and the action.
   456	>
   457	> **## Acceptance criteria**
   458	>
   459	> Two columns, deliberately: **tool-verified** means a named check inside the tool proves the claim, **agent-asserted** means the building agent proves it and says so. A criterion is never left implying a tool checks something it does not.
   460	>

6. **NITPICK — The machine PASS results do not assess the visible snackbar state.** Both snackbars are created only after interaction ([Arm A](</private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-accunliffe-projects-raven-mcp/597ce6a8-fd03-4b4e-badf-6f00d1dc327e/scratchpad/pregate/arm-a/index.html:320>), [Arm B](</private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-accunliffe-projects-raven-mcp/597ce6a8-fd03-4b4e-badf-6f00d1dc327e/scratchpad/pregate/arm-b/index.html:263>)), while the judge submits static HTML without interactions ([judge.mjs](</private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-accunliffe-projects-raven-mcp/597ce6a8-fd03-4b4e-badf-6f00d1dc327e/scratchpad/pregate/judge.mjs:17>)). This weakens the machine evidence but strengthens, rather than refutes, the need for visual evaluation.

7. **NITPICK — N=1 does not independently violate this spec.** The falsifier explicitly describes a singular comparison and supplies no repetition threshold ([spec-pattern-library.md](/Users/accunliffe/projects/raven-mcp/docs/spec-pattern-library.md:555)).
